AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Diagnosis\n\n**Root cause:** the contextual action bar (CAB) outlives the fragment that owns it.\n\n- The tabs are pages of a `ViewPager` backed by a `FragmentStatePagerAdapter` (`MainPagerAdapter`) with the default off-screen page limit of 1.\n- Long-clicking a traveler in `ParticipantTabActivity` (tab 0) starts an `ActionMode` whose callback is an inner class of that fragment. The `ActionMode` itself is owned by the *activity* (`TrickyTripperActivity.mActionMode`), so it keeps showing after the user swipes away.\n- Swiping to "Report" (tab 2) makes tab 0 exceed the off-screen limit, so the pager destroys/detaches the `ParticipantTabActivity` fragment — but the CAB is still on screen.\n- Clicking "create payment" then invokes `MyActionModeCallback.onActionItemClicked` on the detached fragment → `getApp()` calls `getActivity().getApplication()` with `getActivity() == null` → exactly the reported NPE (`ParticipantTabActivity.java:96` = the `getApp()` line, called from line 154 = `option_participant_create_payment`).\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `ParticipantTabActivity` (and the identical pattern in `PaymentTabActivity`):\n\n1. **Track the mode the fragment started** (`startedActionMode`) and **finish it when the page is no longer the visible one** (`setUserVisibleHint(false)`, called by the pager on the outgoing page) and when the fragment\'s view is torn down (`onDestroyView`, i.e. when the pager destroys the off-screen page). This closes the CAB while the activity is still attached, so the stale callback can never be reached — and it also matches normal CAB semantics, since the selection it refers to is no longer visible.\n2. **Defensive guards** so the crash class is eliminated even if a callback still arrives late: `onActionItemClicked` finishes the mode and returns instead of acting on a dead selection; `getApp()`, `getRunningActionMode()` and `setRunningActionMode()` are null-safe; `onDestroyActionMode` clears the stored mode and selection.\n\nExisting behavior is preserved: all menu actions work exactly as before while the tab is visible; only the impossible-to-service case is short-circuited.\n\nVerified with `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` — build succeeds and produces `app/build/outputs/apk/app-debug.apk`.')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 600, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 35649, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 600, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 1, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfHuDXyQ36hKUu3C5bPTv', stop_reason=None, session_id='3ff8fa33-7609-4cd1-b865-eb4565d4d3f4', uuid='42bcff32-1c48-4a9c-af7c-31c61f935d93')
